- **hafiza**: Kerem'in kalıcı çalışma hafızasını yükler ve günceller. Kerem'in repoları, daily-loop otomasyonu, kişisel web sitesi, ESG/iklim/finans araçları, kariyeri, kurulu araçları ya da tercihleri hakkında herhangi bir iş yapılacaksa; "nerede kalmıştık", "devam", "hatırlıyor musun", "bunu hatırla", "hafızaya ekle" dendiğinde; ya da yeni bir sohbet önceki bir sohbetin işine devam ediyorsa kullan. Önce hafızayı oku, sonra işe başla. ([Keremozdemirra/agent-kit](https://github.com/Keremozdemirra/agent-kit/tree/HEAD/archive/skills/hafiza))
- **hafiza-guncelle**: Oturumda öğrenilenleri kalıcı hafızaya işler: yeni tercihler, proje bilgileri, kısaltmalar, kararlar. "Bunu hatırla", "hafızaya ekle", "bunu not al", "bir daha sormana gerek kalmasın" dendiğinde veya uzun bir oturumun sonunda kullan. Also triggers on "remember this", "add this to memory", "save this so I don't have to repeat it", "note that down for next time". Tek seferlik görev detayı, geçici durum veya şifre ve API anahtarı gibi hassas veri için kullanma. ([Keremozdemirra/agent-kit](https://github.com/Keremozdemirra/agent-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/hafiza-guncelle))

- **hafness**: Working in a repo that has a .hafness/ directory. Routes to the harness map and prints the resume point before any other file is read. ([hafshy/hafness](https://github.com/hafshy/hafness/tree/HEAD/skills/hafness))
- **hafness-board**: Publish the Hafness board as a private claude.ai link, or update the existing one. Only on request - the loop never publishes, because publishing makes the agent read the whole board. Usage - /hafness-board ([hafshy/hafness](https://github.com/hafshy/hafness/tree/HEAD/skills/hafness-board))
- **hafness-describe**: Turn what the operator wants into a spec (what and why, never files) on the board. Usage - /hafness-describe &lt;what you want, in your own words&gt; ([hafshy/hafness](https://github.com/hafshy/hafness/tree/HEAD/skills/hafness-describe))
- **hafness-feedback**: Optional. Turn the operator's feedback on done work into new scoped tasks on the board, reopening the spec. Usage - /hafness-feedback F-nnn &lt;what you saw or want changed&gt; ([hafshy/hafness](https://github.com/hafshy/hafness/tree/HEAD/skills/hafness-feedback))
- **hafness-implement**: Build a planned spec task by task inside each task's scope, run the logged gate; a pass makes it done. Usage - /hafness-implement F-nnn ([hafshy/hafness](https://github.com/hafshy/hafness/tree/HEAD/skills/hafness-implement))
- **hafness-init**: Install the Hafness harness into this project — copy .hafness/, detect the stack, write profile.sh, install the history and board hooks, draw the first board, then stop for review. ([hafshy/hafness](https://github.com/hafshy/hafness/tree/HEAD/skills/hafness-init))
- **hafness-plan**: Plan how to build one spec — approach, files, risks — and break it into scoped tasks on the board. Usage - /hafness-plan F-nnn ([hafshy/hafness](https://github.com/hafshy/hafness/tree/HEAD/skills/hafness-plan))
- **hafness-research**: Research a topic (web, docs, this codebase) into a short note on the board. Optional to type - /hafness-describe and /hafness-plan research on their own when a request needs it. Usage - /hafness-research &lt;topic&gt; ([hafshy/hafness](https://github.com/hafshy/hafness/tree/HEAD/skills/hafness-research))
